For example, since night arises after the sun has set, the sun is the cause of the birth of night; that is, night is born from the sun. In the same way, the Uṣas time is born from night, and the sun rises from the Uṣas time. This very idea is described in this Ṛk: as night was born from the sun, so the Uṣas time came into being from night.

(The introduction is ended.)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> महे नो आद्येति दशर्चं सप्तमं सूक्तमात्रेयस्य सत्यश्रवस आर्षं
> पांक्तमुषस्यं । अनुक्रम्यते च । महे दश सत्यश्रवा उषस्यं तु पांक्तमिति ॥
> प्रातरनुवाक उषस्ये क्रत्वा पांक्ते छंदस्याश्विनशस्त्रे चेदं सूक्तं । सूत्र्यते हि ।
> महे नो आद्येति पांक्तं । अ. ४-१४ । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

This sūkta beginning महे नो आद्येति (*mahe no ādyeti*) has ten Ṛks and is the seventh sūkta. Its seer is Satyaśravas of the Atri line, its deity is Uṣas and its metre is Pāṅkta (Paṅkti). The Anukramaṇikā says: महे दश सत्यश्रवा उषस्यं तु पांक्तमिति (*mahe daśa satyaśravā uṣasyaṃ tu pāṃktamiti*). In the Prātaranuvāka, in the Uṣasya rite, in the Pāṅkta metre and in the Āśvina śastra this sūkta is employed. This is laid down in the sūtra: महे नो आद्येति पांक्तं (*mahe no ādyeti pāṃktaṃ*) (A. 4-14). **NOTE**

##### Anuvāda

This sūkta beginning महे नो अद्य (*mahe no adya*) is the seventh sūkta in the sixth anuvāka. It has ten Ṛks. The seer of this sūkta is the one called Satyaśravas Ātreya; the deity is Uṣas; the metre is Paṅkti. In the Anukramaṇikā it is said महे दश सत्यश्रवा उषस्यं तु पांक्तमिति ॥ प्रातरनुवाक उषस्ये क्रत्वा पांक्ते छंदस्याश्विनशस्त्रे चेदं सूक्तं । सूत्र्यते हि । महे नो आद्येति पांक्तं । अ. ४-१४ । इति ॥ अनुवादवु—महे नो अद्य ऎंब ई सूक्तवु आरनॆय अनुवाकदल्लि एळनॆय सूक्तवु. इदरल्लि हत्तु ऋक्कुगळिरुववु. ई सूक्तक्कॆ सत्यश्रवा आत्रेयः ऎंबुवनु ऋषियु; उषोदेवतॆयु देवतॆयु ; पुक्तिश्चंदस्सु, अनुक्रमणिकॆयल्लि महे दश सत्यश्रवा उषस्यं तु पांक्तं (*mahe daśa satyaśravā uṣasyaṃ tu pāṃktamiti ‖ prātaranuvāka uṣasye kratvā pāṃkte chaṃdasyāśvinaśastre cedaṃ sūktaṃ | sūtryate hi | mahe no ādyeti pāṃktaṃ | a. 4-14 | iti ‖ anuvādavu—mahe no adya èṃba ī sūktavu āranèya anuvākadalli eḷanèya sūktavu. idaralli hattu ṛkkugaḷiruvavu. ī sūktakkè satyaśravā ātreyaḥ èṃbuvanu ṛṣiyu; uṣodevatèyu devatèyu ; puktiścaṃdassu, anukramaṇikèyalli mahe daśa satyaśravā uṣasyaṃ tu pāṃktaṃ*). At the time of the recitation of the mantras of the Prātaranuvāka, this sūkta is employed for the mantras in the Paṅkti metre connected with the Uṣasya sacrificial rite

### Printed p. 155

and for the mantras of the Āśvina śastra, as is set out in the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrautasūtra महे नो अद्येति पांक्तं (*mahe no adyeti pāṃktaṃ*) (Ā. 4-14).

##### Particulars of the sūkta

- Maṇḍala 5; Anuvāka 6; Sūkta 79
- Aṣṭaka 4; Adhyāya 4; Varga 21, 22
- Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 10
- Seer: सत्यश्रव अत्रेयः (*satyaśrava atreyaḥ*)
- Deity: उषाः (*uṣāḥ*)
- Metre: पंक्तिः (*paṃktiḥ*)

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> म॒हे नो॑ अ॒द्य बो॑ध॒योषो॑ रा॒ये दि॒वित्म॑ती ।  
> यथा॑ चिन्नो॒ आबो॑धयः स॒त्यश्र॑वसि वा॒य्कॆ सुजा॑ते॒ अश्व॑सूनृते ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> महे । नः । अद्य । बोधय । उषः । राये । दिवित्कृती ।  
> यथा । चित् । नः । अबोधयः । सत्यश्रवसि । वाय्ये । सुजाते । अश्वसूनृते ॥१॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अद्यास्मिन्यागदिने हे उष उषोदेवि दिवित्कृती दीप्तिमती त्वं नोऽस्मान्महे
> महते राये धनप्राप्तये बोधय । प्रज्ञापय । प्रकाशयेत्यर्थः । सति प्रकाशे
> क्रतुद्वारा द्रव्यस्योपार्जयितुं शक्यत्वात् । यथा चित् यथैव पूर्वं सोऽस्मानबोधयः अतीतेषु दिवसेषु यथा बोधितवती तद्वदद्यापीत्यर्थः । हे सुजाते
> शोभनप्रादुर्भूते अश्वसूनृते आश्वार्था प्रियसत्यात्मिका स्तुतिवाग्यस्याः सा । हे
> तादृशि देवि वाय्कॆ वय्यपुत्रे सत्यश्रवसि मय्यनुगृहाणेत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Today, on this sacrificial day, O Uṣas, goddess of light, you awaken us for great wealth, that is, you make us aware and illumine us; for when there is light, it is possible to acquire wealth through the sacrifice. यथा चित् (*yathā cit*): just as you awakened us before, on the days gone by, so also now today. O well-born one, you who have arisen in beauty, O अश्वसूनृते (*aśvasūnṛte*), you whose praise-speech, pleasing and true, is for the sake of horses: O goddess of such a kind, show favour to me, Satyaśravas, the son of Vayya.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- दीप्तिमती (*dīptimatī*)—O radiant one
- उषः (*uṣaḥ*)—O goddess Uṣas
- यथा चित् (*yathā cit*)—in which manner
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- अबोधयः (*abodhayaḥ*)—you awakened (before; in the same manner)
- आद्य (*ādya*)—this sacrificial day also

### Printed p. 156

- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- महे (*mahe*)—abundant
- राये (*rāye*)—for the gain of wealth
- बोधय (*bodhaya*)—awaken
- सुजाते (*sujāte*)—O you of excellent birth
- अश्वसूनृते (*aśvasūnṛte*)—O you who are praised excellently for the sake of horses, O Uṣas
- वाय्ये (*vāyye*)—the son of Vayya
- सत्यश्रवसि (*satyaśravasi*)—in me, who bear the name Satyaśravas, place your favour

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O radiant goddess Uṣas, in the same way as you awakened us all the time before, awaken us this sacrificial day also for the gain of abundant wealth. O you of excellent birth, you who are praised excellently for the sake of horses, O goddess Uṣas, place your favour in me, the son of Vayya, named Satyaśravas.

##### English translation
Radiant Ushas, wake us up to-day for (the acquisition of) ample riches, in like manner as you have awakened us (of old): bright-born, and praised sincerely for (the gift of) horses, shew favour to Satyashravas, the son of Vayya.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In this Ṛk and in the next two Ṛks, the seer of this sūkta, Satyaśravas, has said of himself that he is the son of one called Vayya. In some places it is said that Vayya was a royal sage and that the Aśvin deities protected him.
याभिः कर्कंधुं वय्यं च जिन्नथस्ताभिरू षु ऊतिभिरश्विना गतं ॥ (*yābhiḥ karkaṃdhuṃ vayyaṃ ca jinnathastābhirū ṣu ūtibhiraśvinā gataṃ ‖*) (Ṛ. Saṃ. 1-112-6) **GLOSS**
In the commentary on this Ṛk the commentator has spoken thus:
कर्कंधुः वय्यश्च राजर्षि अतिक्षात् तपस्तप्यमानौ यदृच्छया पर्यटंतावश्विन् ददृशतुः । दृष्ट्वा च तावमृतेनाप्याययांचक्रतुरिति । तदेतदिहोच्यते ॥ (*karkaṃdhuḥ vayyaśca rājarṣi atikṣāt tapastapyamānau yadṛcchayā paryaṭaṃtāvaśvin dadṛśatuḥ | dṛṣṭvā ca tāvamṛtenāpyāyayāṃcakraturiti | tadetadihocyate ‖*)
The royal sages Karkandhu and Vayya, while practising extremely painful austerity and wandering in the forest, were seen by the Aśvin deities by chance. Afterwards the Aśvins satisfied them with the gift of nectar: so it is said. The matter of Karkandhu is not spoken of anywhere else in the Ṛgveda except in this Ṛk. But the fact that Vayya received help not only from the Aśvin deities but also from Indra and the other deities is indicated elsewhere:

### Printed p. 157

त्वमाविथ नर्यं तुर्वशं यदुं त्वं तुर्वितिं वय्यं शतक्रतो । (*tvamāvitha naryaṃ turvaśaṃ yaduṃ tvaṃ turvitiṃ vayyaṃ śatakrato |*) (Ṛ. Saṃ. 1-54-6) **GLOSS**
अरमयः सरपसस्तराय कं तुर्वीतये च वय्याय च स्तुति । (*aramayaḥ sarapasastarāya kaṃ turvītaye ca vayyāya ca stuti |*) (Ṛ. Saṃ. 2-13-12) **GLOSS**
त्वं महीमवनिं विश्वधेनां तुर्वीतये वय्याय क्षरंतीं । (*tvaṃ mahīmavaniṃ viśvadhenāṃ turvītaye vayyāya kṣaraṃtīṃ |*) (Ṛ. Saṃ. 4-19-6) **GLOSS**
In these and other Ṛks the matter of Vayya is stated. For the word वय्यं (*vayyaṃ*) in the Ṛk Ṛ. Saṃ. 1-54-6, Sāyaṇa explains: वय्यकुलजं तुर्वितिनामानं (*vayyakulajaṃ turvitināmānaṃ*), that is, a king named Turviti, born in the family of Vayya. This shows that Vayya, and the other royal sages born in his family, were famous.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> या सु॑नी॒थे शौ॑चद्र॒थे वाच्यो॑ दुहितर्दिवः ।  
> सा व्यु॑च्छ॒ सही॑यसि स॒त्यश्र॑वसि वा॒य्ये सुजा॑ते॒ अश्व॑सूनृते ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> या । सुनीथे । शौचत् रथे । वि । औच्छः । दुहितः । दिवः ।  
> सा । वि । उच्छ । सहीयसि । सत्यश्रवसि । वाय्ये । सुजाते । अश्वसूनृते ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे दिवो दुहितर्दिवः सूर्यस्य पुत्रुषो या त्वं सुनीथ एतन्नामके
> शौचद्रथे शुचद्रथस्यापत्ये पूर्वं व्यौच्छृ व्यवासयस्तमांसि सा त्वं सहीयस्यतिशयेन बलवति वाय्मे वय्यपुत्रे सत्यश्रवसि मयि व्युच्छ । तमो विवासय । उच्छी
> विवासे । विवासो वर्जनं । शिष्टमुक्तं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O daughter of heaven, Uṣas, you who earlier dispelled the darkness for Sunītha, son of Śucadratha, **NOTE** that same you, O very powerful one, dawn upon me, Satyaśravas, the son of Vayya; drive away the darkness. The root उच्छी (*ucchī*) is in the sense विवासे (*vivāse*), and विवास (*vivāsa*) means removal. The rest has been explained.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—of the sky
- दुहितः (*duhitaḥ*)—O Uṣas, daughter
- या (*yā*)—you who
- शौचद्रथे (*śaucadrathe*)—son of Śucadratha
- सुनीथे (*sunīthe*)—for Sunītha
- व्याच्छः (*vyācchaḥ*)—have shone, destroying the darkness

### Printed p. 158

- सुजाते (*sujāte*)—O you of excellent birth
- अश्वसूनृते (*aśvasūnṛte*)—O you who are praised excellently for the sake of horses
- सा (*sā*)—that same you
- वाय्कॆ (*vāykè*)—the son of Vayya
- सहीयसि (*sahīyasi*)—possessed of strength
- सत्यश्रवसि (*satyaśravasi*)—bearing the name Satyaśravas, for me
- व्युच्छ्र (*vyucchra*)—shine

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Uṣas, daughter of the sky, you who formerly shone, destroying the darkness, for Sunītha, the son of Śucadratha, O you of excellent birth, you who are praised excellently for the sake of horses, that same you shine for me, the son of Vayya, strong, named Satyaśravas.

##### English translation
Daughter of heaven, who have dawned upon Sunitha, the son of Shuchadratha, bright-born, and praised sincerely for (the gift of) horses, dawn upon the powerful son of Vayya, Satyashravas.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

शौचद्रथे सुनीथे (*śaucadrathe sunīthe*)—Śucadratha and his son called Sunītha may have belonged to the family of the famous king named Vayya, and may have been forefathers or relatives of Satyaśravas, the seer of this mantra. Although the word सुनीथ (*sunītha*) is recited in the Ṛks Ṛ. Saṃ. 1-35-7; 1-35-10; 2-8-2; 7-46-4; 10-47-2; 3-8-8; 6-51-11; 1-62-3; 5-67-4, the commentator has explained this word as सुनयन (*sunayana*). Not much can be learned about this Sunītha. **NOTE**

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> सा नो॑ अ॒द्याभ॒रद्व॑ सु॒व्यू॑च्छा दुहितर्दिवः ।  
> यो व्याच्छः॒ सही॑यसि स॒त्यश्र॑वसि वा॒य्ये सुजा॑ते॒ अश्व॑सूनृते॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> सा । नः । आद्य । आभरत् वसुः । वि । उच्छ । दुहितः । दिवः ।  
> यो इति । वि । औच्छः । सहीयसि । सत्यश्रवसि । वाय्मे । सुजाते । अश्वसूनृते ॥३॥

### Printed p. 159

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे दुहितर्दिव उष आभरद्वसुराहृतधना सा प्रसिद्धा त्वं नोऽस्माकमद्यास्मिन्निने व्युच्छ । तपो विवासय । हे सहीयसि यो या । उकारोऽनर्थकः । या
> त्वं पूर्वं व्यौच्छः साद्यापीति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O daughter of heaven, Uṣas, you who are आभरद्वसुः (*ābharadvasuḥ*), one who has brought wealth: you, the famous one, shine for us today, on this day; drive away the darkness. **NOTE** O very powerful one, यो (*yo*) means या (*yā*) ("who"); the vowel उ (*u*) has no meaning. You who dawned before, that same you do so today also.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- आभरद्वसुः (*ābharadvasuḥ*)—bringer of wealth
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—of the sky
- दुहितः (*duhitaḥ*)—daughter, O goddess Uṣas
- यो (*yo*) (या (*yā*))—you who
- सुजाते (*sujāte*)—O you of excellent birth
- अश्वशूनृते (*aśvaśūnṛte*)—O you who are prayed to for the sake of horses
- वाय्कॆ (*vāykè*)—the son of Vayya
- सहीयसि (*sahīyasi*)—possessed of strength
- सत्यश्रवसि (*satyaśravasi*)—for Satyaśravas
- व्याच्छः (*vyācchaḥ*)—you have shone, destroying the darkness
- सा (*sā*)—that same glorious you
- अद्य (*adya*)—this day of the sacrifice
- नः (*naḥ*)—for us
- व्यच्छ (*vyaccha*)—shine

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Uṣas, bringer of wealth and daughter of the sky, you who, being of excellent birth and prayed to for the sake of horses, shone, destroying the darkness, for Satyaśravas, the son of Vayya and possessed of strength: that same glorious you, shine for us this day of the sacrifice.

##### English translation
Daughter of heaven, you are the bringer of opulence ; dawn upon us to-day, as bright-born, and praised for (the gift of) horses ; you have dawned upon the powerful Satyashravas, the son of Vayya.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒भि ये त्वा॑ विभावरि॒ स्तोमै॑गृ॒णंति॒ वह्न॑यः ।  
> म॒घैर्म॑घोनि सु॒त्रियो॒ दाम॑न्वंतः सुरा॒तयः॒ सुजा॑ते॒अश्व॑सूनृते ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अभि । योः । त्वा । विभावरि । स्तोमैः । गृणंति । वह्नयः ॥  
> मघैः। मघोनि। सुश्रियः। दामन्वंतः। सुरातयः। सुजाते। अश्वसूनृते॥४॥

### Printed p. 160

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे विभावरि प्रकाशोपेते ॥ विभाशब्दाच्छंदसीवनिपाविति वनिप् । भातीर्वा
> कर्तरि वनिप् ॥ उषस्त्वा त्वामभि प्रति ये वह्नयो प्रोढार ऋत्विजः स्तोतारः
> स्तोमैः स्तोत्रैर्ग्गणंति स्तुवंति ते स्तोतारो मघैर्हॆ मघोनि धनैर्धनवति
> त्वदनुग्रहात् सुश्रियो भवंति । सुष्पर्थिभिराश्रयणीया भवंति । दामन्वंतो
> दानवंतो भवंति । सुरातयः । रातिर्दानं । सुदानाश्च भवंति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Vibhāvarī, you who are endowed with light. Those वह्नयः (*vahnayaḥ*), the carriers, that is, the priests and singers, who praise you, Uṣas, with स्तोमैः (*stomaiḥ*), hymns of praise, those singers become, O wealthy one, rich in wealth through your favour; they become well-endowed, ones to be resorted to by those who seek, **NOTE** givers of gifts, and liberal givers; राति (*rāti*) means a gift.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- मघोनि (*maghoni*)—O you who are endowed with wealth
- सुजाते (*sujāte*)—O you of excellent birth
- अश्वसूनृते (*aśvasūnṛte*)—O you who are praised excellently for the sake of horses
- विभावरि (*vibhāvari*)—O radiant goddess Uṣas
- त्वा (*tvā*) अभि (*abhi*)—you
- ये (*ye*)—which
- वह्नयः (*vahnayaḥ*)—priests
- स्तोमैः (*stomaiḥ*)—with hymns of praise
- गृणंति (*gṛṇaṃti*)—praise, they
- मघैः (*maghaiḥ*)—with riches
- सुश्रियः (*suśriyaḥ*)—endowed with wealth
- दामन्वंतः (*dāmanvaṃtaḥ*)—liberal givers
- सुरातयः (*surātayaḥ*)—and offerers of excellent oblations they become

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Uṣas, you who are endowed with wealth, of excellent origin, praised excellently for the sake of horses, and radiant: those priests who praise you with hymns of praise become rich with wealth, liberal givers and offerers of excellent oblations.

##### English translation
The offerers of oblations who eulogize you, lustrous Ushas, with sacred hymns, become prosperous with effluence, (through your favour); bestower of wealth, bright-born goddess, (you are) sincerely praised for (the gift of) horses.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यचि॒द्दि ते॑ ग॒णा इ॒मे छ॒दयं॑ति म॒घत्त॑ये ।  
> परि॑ चि॒द्वष्ट॑यो दधु॒र्दद॑तो॒ राथो॒ अह्र॑यं॒ सुजा॑ते॒  ॑
> अश्वसूनृते ॥ ५ ॥

### Printed p. 161

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यत् । चित् । हि । ते । गणाः । इमे । छदयंति । मघत्तये ।  
> परि । चित् । वष्टयः । दधुः । ददतः । राधः । अह्रयं । सुजाते । अश्वसूनृते ॥५॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे उषो यच्चिद्दि । हीति पूरणः । यत् ये केचन ते तव स्वभूता इमे
> पुरतो वर्तमाना गणाः संघभूता मघत्तये धनदातये धनदानाय धनवत्वाय वा छदयंति उपच्छंदयंति ते सर्वॆऽप्यस्मान् परि दधुः हरितो धारयंति
> वष्टयोऽस्थानेव कामयमानाः । किं कुर्वंतः । अह्रयमह्रियमाणमक्षीणमलज्जावहं वा राधो धनं हविर्लक्षणं ददतो यजंत इत्यर्थः । अर्थिभ्योऽक्षीणं
> ददतो वा। ये त्वां हविर्ददतः स्तुवंति ते सर्वॆऽप्यस्मदर्थमेव फलं धारयंति ।
> स्तुवंतीत्यर्थः । अथवा यद्दीत्यत्र यद्यपीति व्याख्येयं । तस्मिन् पक्ष उत्तरवाक्ये
> तथापीत्यध्याहार्यं । अवशिष्टं समानं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Uṣas, यच्चिद्दि (*yacciddi*): the word हि (*hi*) is a filler. Whatever these groups of your own, standing before you, assembled together, छदयंति (*chadayaṃti*), invite, for the sake of मघत्तये (*maghattaye*), that is, for giving wealth, for the bestowal of wealth, or for the possession of wealth, all of them परि दधुः (*pari dadhuḥ*), hold us round about, **NOTE** वष्टयः (*vaṣṭayaḥ*), desiring us. **NOTE** What are they doing? Giving अह्रयं (*ahrayaṃ*), that which is not diminished, which is undiminished or does not bring shame, राधः (*rādhaḥ*), wealth in the form of an oblation; that is, they sacrifice; or they give undiminished wealth to those who ask. Those who, giving an oblation, praise you, all of them hold the fruit for our sake alone; that is, they praise. Or else यद्दी (*yaddī*) is to be explained as "even though"; in that alternative the words "even so" are to be supplied in the latter sentence. The rest is the same.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सुजाते (*sujāte*)—O you of excellent birth
- अश्वसूनृते (*aśvasūnṛte*)—O goddess Uṣas, you who are praised excellently for the sake of horses
- ते (*te*)—your devotees
- इमे (*ime*)—those who have gathered here
- यच्छिद्दि (*yacchiddi*) गणाः (*gaṇāḥ*)—whichever companies of sacrificers
- मघत्तये (*maghattaye*)—for the giving of wealth
- छदयंति (*chadayaṃti*)—desire
- ते (*te*)—all of them
- अह्रयं (*ahrayaṃ*)—not small (abundant)
- राधः (*rādhaḥ*)—wealth
- ददतः (*dadataḥ*)—giving to us
- वष्टयः (*vaṣṭayaḥ*)—with the desire to make us alone worthy of that wealth
- परि दधुः (*pari dadhuḥ*)—they attend on us on all sides

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O goddess Uṣas, you of excellent origin and praised excellently for the sake of horses, all those companies of sacrificers, your devotees who have gathered here, who desire to give wealth, give us abundant wealth, and with the desire to make us alone worthy of that wealth, attend on us on all sides.

##### English translation
These, your assembled (worshippers), who stand before you to distribute wealth, entertain towards us kindly intentions, offering unlimited

### Printed p. 162

riches: bright-born goddess, (you are) sincerely praised for (the gift of horses.

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ऐषु॑ धा वी॒रव॒द्यश॒ उषो॑ मघोनि सू॒रिषु॑ ।  
> ये नो॒ राधां॒स्य प्र॑या म॒घवा॑नो॒ अरा॑सत॒ सुजा॑ते॒ अश्व॑सूनृते ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । एषु । धाः । वीरवत् । यशः । उषः । मघोनि । सूरिषु ।  
> ये । नः । राधांसि । अह्रया । मघ्नानः । आरासत । सुजाते । अश्वसूनृते ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे उषो देवि मघोनि मघवति त्वमेषु सूरिम स्तोतृषु वा वीरवद्य.
> शो वीरैः पुत्रादिभिरुपेतमन्नं यशो वा आ धाः । आधेहि । आदेहि । आनय ।
> ये मघवानो धनवंतो नूरयो नोऽस्मभ्यं तव स्तोतृभ्यो राधांसि
> धनान्यह्रयाक्षीणान्यरासत ददुः तेष्टित्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O goddess Uṣas, wealthy one, bestow on these singers, or on these praisers, वीरवत् (*vīravat*) यशः (*yaśaḥ*), food, or fame, accompanied by heroes, sons and the like; आ धाः (*ā dhāḥ*) means place, give, bring. Those wealthy and rich givers who have given us, your praisers, undiminished riches, अरासत (*arāsata*), gave: for them, this is the meaning. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सुजाते (*sujāte*)—O you of excellent birth
- अश्वसूनृते (*aśvasūnṛte*)—O you who are prayed to with excellent praises for the sake of horses
- मघोनि (*maghoni*)—O wealthy one
- उषः (*uṣaḥ*)—O goddess Uṣas
- मघवानः (*maghavānaḥ*)—wealthy
- ये (*ye*)—which of your praisers
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- अह्रया (*ahrayā*)—not small
- राधांसि (*rādhāṃsi*)—riches
- अरासत (*arāsata*)—give
- एषु (*eṣu*)—such
- सूरिषु (*sūriṣu*)—among these singers
- वीरवत् (*vīravat*)—accompanied by heroic sons and the like
- यशः (*yaśaḥ*)—food
- आ धाः (*ā dhāḥ*)—fill

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O goddess Uṣas, of excellent birth, prayed to with excellent praises for the sake of horses, and wealthy: whichever wealthy praisers of yours give us abundant riches, fill such singers with food accompanied by heroic sons and the like.

### Printed p. 163

##### English translation
Affluent Ushas, bestow upon these (your) devout adorers food and posterity, so that, being opulent, they may, without stint, bestow riches upon us: bright-born goddess (who are) sincerely praised for (the gift of) horses.

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> तेभ्यो॑ द्यु॒म्नं बृ॒हद्यश॒ उषो॑ मघो॒न्या व॑ह ।  
> ये नो॒ राधां॒स्यश्वा॑ ग॒व्या भजं॑त सू॒रयः॒ सुजा॑ते॒ अश्व॑सूनृते ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> तेभ्यः । द्युम्नं । बृहत् । यशः । उषः । मघोनि । आ । वह ।  
> ये । नः । राधांसि । अश्वा । गव्या । भजंत । सूरयः । सुजाते । अश्वसूनृते ॥७॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे उषो मघोनि त्वं तेभ्यो द्युम्नं द्योतमानं हिरण्यादिरूपं धनं
> बृहद्यशो महदन्नं महतीं कीर्तिं वा तेभ्यो यजमानेभ्य आ वह । य आध्या
> नोऽस्मभ्यं राधांसि धनान्यश्वाश्चैर्युक्तानि गव्या गोभिर्युक्तानि गोसमूहानश्वसमूहान् च भजंत भजेरन् ददुः सूरयो दातारः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Uṣas, wealthy one, bring to those sacrificers द्युम्नं (*dyumnaṃ*), shining wealth in the form of gold and the like, and बृहद्यशो (*bṛhadyaśo*), great food or great fame. Those who give us riches, wealth joined with horses, and गव्या (*gavyā*), joined with cows, herds of cows and herds of horses, भजंत (*bhajaṃta*), they would give; सूरयः (*sūrayaḥ*) means the givers. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सुजाते (*sujāte*)—O you of excellent birth
- अश्वसूनृते (*aśvasūnṛte*)—O you who are prayed to with excellent praises for the sake of horses
- मघोनि (*maghoni*)—O wealthy one
- उषः (*uṣaḥ*)—O goddess Uṣas
- ये (*ye*)—which
- सूरयः (*sūrayaḥ*)—praisers who are givers
- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- अश्वा (*aśvā*)—possessed of horses
- गव्या (*gavyā*)—possessed of cows
- राधांसि (*rādhāṃsi*)—riches
- भजंत (*bhajaṃta*)—give
- तेभ्यः (*tebhyaḥ*)—to them
- द्युम्नं (*dyumnaṃ*)—shining gold and the like
- बृहत् (*bṛhat*)—abundant
- यशः (*yaśaḥ*)—food
- आ वह (*ā vaha*)—bring

### Printed p. 164

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O goddess Uṣas, of excellent birth, prayed to with excellent praises for the sake of horses, and wealthy: to those praisers who are liberal givers and who give us riches possessed of horses and possessed of cows, bring shining gold and the like and abundant food.

##### English translation
Affluent Ushas, bring wealth and abundant food to those who, liberal givers, bestow upon us riches with horses and cattle : bright-born goddess (you are) sincerely praised for (the gift of ) horses.

#### Ṛk 8

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> उ॒त नो॒ गोम॑ती॒रिष॒ आ व॑हा दुहितर्दिवः ।  
> सा॒कं सूर्य॑स्य र॒त्मिभिः॑ शु॒क्रैः शोच॑द्भिर॒र्चिभिः॒ सुजा॑ते॒ अश्व॑सूनृते ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> उत । नः । गोमतीः । इषः । आ । वह । दुहितः । दिवः ।  
> साकं । सूर्यस्य । रश्मिभिः । शुक्रैः । शोचत्भिः । अर्चिभिः । सुजाते ।  
> अश्वसूनृते ॥ ८ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> उतासि च नोऽस्मभ्यं गोमतीर्गोभिरुपेतानीपोऽन्नान्या वह आनय हे
> दिवो दुहितरुषः । कदा । सूर्यस्य रश्मिभिः साकं शोचद्भिर्दिर्पयद्भिः शुक्रैर्निर्मलैरचिभिरग्नेस्तेजोभिश्च साकं । सूर्योदयकालेऽग्नींधनकाले चेत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

And bring to us, O Uṣas, daughter of heaven, गोमतीः इषः (*gomatīḥ iṣaḥ*), foods accompanied by cattle. When? Together with the rays of the sun, and together with the blazing, that is, shining, pure and bright flames of fire, the splendours of fire; that is, at the time of sunrise and at the time of kindling the fire.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सुजाते (*sujāte*)—O you of excellent birth
- अश्वसूनृते (*aśvasūnṛte*)—O you who are praised with excellent praises for the sake of horses
- दिवः दुहितः (*divaḥ duhitaḥ*)—O goddess Uṣas, daughter of the sky

### Printed p. 165

- नः (*naḥ*)—to us
- सूर्यस्य (*sūryasya*)—of the sun
- शुक्रैः (*śukraiḥ*)—shining
- रश्मिभिः साकं (*raśmibhiḥ sākaṃ*)—together with the rays
- शोचद्भिः (*śocadbhiḥ*)—blazing and radiant
- अर्चिभिः (*arcibhiḥ*)—also with the flames of fire
- गोमतीः (*gomatīḥ*)—accompanied by cattle
- इषः (*iṣaḥ*)—foods
- आ वह (*ā vaha*)—bring

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O goddess Uṣas, of excellent birth, praised with excellent praises for the sake of horses, and daughter of the sky: bring us foods accompanied by cattle, together with the shining rays of the sun and with the blazing, radiant flames of fire.

##### English translation
Daughter of heaven, bring to us food and cattle. together with the pure rays of the sun, and the radiant flames (of the kindled fires) : bright-born goddess, (you are) sincerely praised for (the gift of ) horses.

#### Ṛk 9

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> व्यु॑च्छा दुहितर्दिवो॒ मा चि॒रं त॑नुथा॒ अपः॑ ।  
> नेत्वा॑ स्ते॒नं यथा॑ रि॒पुं तपा॑ति॒ सूरो॑ अ॒र्चिषा॒ सुजा॑ते॒ अश्व॑सूनृते ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> वि । उच्छ । दुहितः । दिवः । मा । चिरं । तनुथाः । अपः ।  
> न । इत् । त्वा । स्तेनं । यथा । रिपुं । तपाति । सूरः । अर्चिषा । सुजाते । अश्वसूनृते ॥ ९ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे दिवो दुहितरुषः त्वं व्युच्छ । विभातं कुरु । अपोऽस्मदीयं कर्म प्रति
> चिरं विलंबं मा तनुथाः । त्वा त्वां रिपुं स्तेनं यथा संतापयति राजादिस्तद्वत्सूरः
> सूर्योऽर्चिषा तेजसा नेत् नैव तपाति । तप्यात् । शीघ्रं नोदेश्वित्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Uṣas, daughter of heaven, you shine; make it dawn. Do not prolong delay, चिरं (*ciraṃ*), with regard to our rite, अपः (*apaḥ*). Just as a king or the like torments a thief or an enemy, so may the sun not scorch you with his ray, अर्चिषा (*arciṣā*), his brightness. **NOTE**

### Printed p. 166

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सुजाते (*sujāte*)—O you of excellent birth
- अश्वसूनृते (*aśvasūnṛte*)—O you who are praised with excellent praises for the sake of horses
- दिवः दुहितः (*divaḥ duhitaḥ*)—O goddess Uṣas, daughter of the sky
- व्युच्छ (*vyuccha*)—drive away the darkness and shine
- अपः (*apaḥ*)—to our rite
- चिरं (*ciraṃ*)—delay
- मा तनुथाः (*mā tanuthāḥ*)—do not cause
- रिप्रं (*ripraṃ*)—an enemy
- सेनं (*senaṃ*)—and a thief
- यथा (*yathā*)—in the manner in which a king torments
- सूरः (*sūraḥ*)—the sun
- त्वा (*tvā*)—you
- अर्चिषा (*arciṣā*)—with his brightness
- नेत् तपाति (*net tapāti*)—may he certainly not burn and torment

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O goddess Uṣas, of excellent birth, praised with excellent praises for the sake of horses, and daughter of the sky: drive away the darkness and shine for us. Do not cause delay to our rite. In the manner in which a king torments an enemy and a thief, may the sun certainly not burn and torment you with his brightness.

##### English translation
Daughter of heaven, Dawn! delay not our (sacred) rite ; let not the sun scorch you with his ray, as (a prince punishes) a thief, or (subdues) an enemy: bright-born goddess, (you are) sincerely praised for (the gift of) horses.

#### Ṛk 10

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ए॒ताव॒द्वेदु॑ष॒स्त्वं भूयो॑ वा॒ दातु॑मर्हसि ।  
> या स्तो॒तृभ्यो॑ विभावर्यु॒च्छंती॒ न प्र॒माय॑से॒ सुजा॑ते॒  ॑
> अश्वसून्यते ॥ १० ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> एतावत् । वा । इत् । उषः । त्वं । भूयः । वा । दातुं । अर्हसि ।  
> या । स्तोतृभ्यः । विभावरि । उच्छंती । न । प्रमीयसे । सुजाते । अश्वसूनृते ॥ १० ॥

### Printed p. 167

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे उषस्त्वमस्मभ्यं वाथवा दित्सितं किमेतावदेव । इच्छब्द एवकारार्थः ।
> अस्मिन् सूक्ते प्रार्थितमेवेत्यर्थः । अथवा भूयो दातुमर्हसि । अप्रार्थितमपि ।
> यद्दातव्यमस्ति तत्सर्वं देहीत्यर्थः । हे विभावर्युषो या त्वं स्तोतृभ्यस्तेषां
> लौकिकवैदिकव्यवहारार्थं व्युच्छंती तमो विवासयंती न प्रमीयसे । न हंसि । न
> क्रुद्धसीत्यर्थः । सा त्वं दातुमर्हसीति ॥ मीञ् हिंसायामित्यस्मात् श्नास्थाने
> व्यत्ययेन श्यन् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Uṣas, is what you wish to give us only this much? The word इच्छब्द (*icchabda*) has the sense of "only": what has been prayed for in this sūkta alone, this is the meaning. Or else you are able to give more, even what has not been prayed for; whatever is to be given, give all of it, this is the meaning. O radiant Uṣas, you who, dawning for the singers, driving away the darkness for the sake of their worldly and Vedic activity, न प्रमीयसे (*na pramīyase*), are not harmful to them: you do not injure them; you are not angry, this is the meaning. Such as you are, you are able to give.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- सुजाते (*sujāte*)—O you of excellent birth
- अश्वसूनृते (*aśvasūnṛte*)—O you who are praised with excellent praises for the sake of horses
- विभावरि (*vibhāvari*)—O radiant
- उषः (*uṣaḥ*)—goddess Uṣas
- या (*yā*)—you who
- स्तोतृभ्यः (*stotṛbhyaḥ*)—for the praisers
- उच्छंती (*ucchaṃtī*)—destroying the darkness
- न प्रमीयसे (*na pramīyase*)—are not harmful to them
- त्वं (*tvaṃ*)—such you
- एतावदित् (*etāvadit*)—just as much as is specified in this prayer
- ना (*nā*)—or **NOTE**
- भूयं वा (*bhūyaṃ vā*)—or in a still greater measure
- दातुं (*dātuṃ*)—to give
- अर्हसि (*arhasi*)—you are able

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O radiant goddess Uṣas, of excellent origin, praised with excellent praises for the sake of horses: you who, destroying the darkness for your praisers, are not harmful to them, such as you are, you are able to give, whether just as much as is specified in this prayer or in a still greater measure.

##### English translation
You, Ushas, are able to give (us) whatever, indeed, (has been solicited), and much (that has not been asked for); for, radiant (divinity), you are dawning upon your adorers, you are never cruel (to them): bright-born goddes (you are) sincerely praised for (the gift of) horses.

## Sūkta 80 (Maṇḍala 5)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> द्युतद्यामानमिति षडृचमष्टमं सूक्तं । तत्रानुक्रमणिका । द्युतद्यामानं
> षडिति । सत्यश्रवा ऋषिः । अनुक्तत्वात् त्रिष्टुप् । उषस्यं त्वित्युक्तत्वादुषा देवता ॥
> प्रातरनुवाक उपस्ये क्रत्वा त्रैष्टुभे छंदस्याश्विनशस्त्रे चेदं सूक्तं । सूत्रितं च ।
> द्युतद्यामानमुषो वाजेन । आ. ४-१४ । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

This sūkta beginning द्युतद्यामानं (*dyutadyāmānaṃ*) has six Ṛks and is the eighth sūkta. The Anukramaṇikā says द्युतद्यामानं (*dyutadyāmānaṃ*) and six. The seer is Satyaśravas. Because no metre is mentioned, it is Triṣṭubh. Because उषस्यं त्वित्युक्तत्वादुषा देवता (*uṣasyaṃ tvityuktatvāduṣā devatā*) is stated, the deity is Uṣas. In the Prātaranuvāka, in the Uṣasya rite, in the Traiṣṭubha metre and in the Āśvina śastra this sūkta is employed. This is laid down in the sūtra: द्युतद्यामानमुषो वाजेन (*dyutadyāmānamuṣo vājena*) (Ā. 4-14).

##### Anuvāda

This sūkta beginning द्युतद्यामानं (*dyutadyāmānaṃ*) is the eighth sūkta in the sixth anuvāka. It has six Ṛks. In the Anukramaṇikā it is said द्युतद्यामानं षट् (*dyutadyāmānaṃ ṣaṭ*). The seer of this sūkta is the one called Satyaśravas; the deity is Uṣas; the metre is Triṣṭubh. At the time of the recitation of the mantras of the Prātaranuvāka, this sūkta is employed for the mantras in the Triṣṭubh metre connected with the Uṣasya sacrificial rite and for the mantras of the Aśvina śastra, as is set out in the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrautasūtra द्युतद्यामानमुषो वाजेन (*dyutadyāmānamuṣo vājena*) (Ā. 4-14). **NOTE**

##### Particulars of the sūkta

- Maṇḍala 5; Anuvāka 6; Sūkta 80
- Aṣṭaka 4; Adhyāya 4; Varga 23
- Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 6
- Seer: सत्यश्रवा आत्रेयः (*satyaśravā ātreyaḥ*)
- Deity: उषाः (*uṣāḥ*)
- Metre: त्रिष्टुप् (*triṣṭup*)

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> द्यु॒तद्या॑मानं बृह॒तीमृ॒तेन॑ ऋ॒ताव॑रीमरु॒णप्सुं॑ विभा॒तीं।  
> दे॒वीमु॒षसं॒ स्व॑रा॒वहं॑तीं॒ प्रति॒ विप्रा॑सो म॒तिभि॑र्जरंते॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> द्युतत्त्यामानं । बृहतीं । ऋतेन । ऋतवरीं । अरुणप्सुं । विभातीं ।  
> देवीं । उषसं । स्वः । आवहंतीं । प्रति । विप्रासः । मतिभिः । जरंते ॥ १ ॥

### Printed p. 169

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> द्युतद्यामानं दीप्तरथां बृहतीं महतीमृतेन सत्येन यज्ञेन वर्तावरीमृतवतीमरुणप्सुमरुणरूपां विभातीं व्युच्छंतीं देवीं द्योतमानामुषसं
> स्वरावहंतीं सूर्यं गच्छंतीं । एवं महानुभावामुषसं विप्रासो मेधाविन
> ऋत्विजो मतिभिः स्तुतिभिः प्रति जरंते । स्तुवंति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

द्युतद्यामानं (*dyutadyāmānaṃ*): having a shining chariot; बृहतीं (*bṛhatīṃ*): great; ऋतेन (*ṛtena*): by truth, by the sacrifice; she is ऋतावरीं (*ṛtāvarīṃ*), possessed of truth; अरुणप्सुं (*aruṇapsuṃ*): of ruddy form; विभातीं (*vibhātīṃ*): dawning; देवीं (*devīṃ*): the shining उषसं (*uṣasaṃ*), Uṣas; स्वरावहंतीं (*svarāvahaṃtīṃ*): going to the sun. Such a Uṣas, of great majesty, the wise priests, विप्रासः (*viprāsaḥ*), मतिभिः (*matibhiḥ*), with hymns of praise, प्रति जरंते (*prati jaraṃte*), praise.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- द्युतद्यामानं (*dyutadyāmānaṃ*)—she who has a shining chariot
- बृहतीं (*bṛhatīṃ*)—she who is of great form
- ऋतेन (*ṛtena*)—by the sacrifice
- ऋतावरीं (*ṛtāvarīṃ*)—she who is worshipped
- अरुणप्सुं (*aruṇapsuṃ*)—she who has a golden-coloured form
- विभातीं (*vibhātīṃ*)—she who shines, dispersing the darkness
- देवीं (*devīṃ*)—she who has the nature of a deity
- स्वरावहंतीं (*svarāvahaṃtīṃ*)—she who goes before the sun
- उषसं (*uṣasaṃ*)—goddess Uṣas
- विप्रासः (*viprāsaḥ*)—wise priests
- मतिभिः (*matibhiḥ*)—with hymns of praise
- प्रति जरंते (*prati jaraṃte*)—praise

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The wise priests praise with hymns of praise the goddess Uṣas, who has a shining chariot, is of great form, is worshipped through the sacrifice, has a golden-coloured form, shines dispersing the darkness, has the nature of a deity and goes before the sun.

##### English translation
The wise priests celebrate with hymns the divine, bright-charioted, expanded Dawn ; worshipped with holy worship, purple-tinted, radiant, leading on the sun.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ए॒षा जनं॑ दर्श॒ता बो॒धयं॑ति सु॒गान्प॒थः कृ॑ण्व॒ती या॒त्यग्रे॑ ।  
> बृ॒ह॒द्र॒था बृ॑ह॒ती वि॑श्वमि॒न्नोषा ज्योति॑र्यच्छ॒त्यग्रे॒ अह्नां॑॥२॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> एषा । जनं । दर्शता । बोधयंती । सुगान् । पथः । कृण्वती । याति । अग्रे ।  
> बृहत्ठरथा । बृहती । विश्वंइन्वा । उषाः । ज्योतिः । यच्छति । आग्रे । अह्नां ॥ २ ॥

### Printed p. 170

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> दर्शता दर्शनीयैषोषा बोधयंती । कं । जनं । प्रसुप्तमित्यर्थः । पथः
> सुगान् सुगमनान् कृण्वत्यग्रे सूर्यस्य पुरस्ताद्याति । गच्छति । कीदृश्युषाः ।
> बृहद्रथा प्रभूतरथा बृहती महती विश्वमिन्वा विश्वं व्याप्नुवाना विश्वतर्पणा ना ।
> ईदृश्युषा अह्नामग्रे ज्योतिस्तेजो यच्छति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

दर्शता (*darśatā*): this lovely Uṣas, बोधयंती (*bodhayaṃtī*), awakening: whom? The person who is asleep, this is the meaning. Making the paths सुगान् (*sugān*), easy to travel, she goes अग्रे (*agre*), before the sun. What is this Uṣas like? बृहद्रथा (*bṛhadrathā*), having a large chariot; बृहती (*bṛhatī*), great; विश्वमिन्वा (*viśvaminvā*), pervading the whole world. Such a Uṣas bestows light, brilliance, at the beginning of the days.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- दर्शता (*darśatā*)—she who has an attractive form
- एषा (*eṣā*)—this Uṣas
- जनं (*janaṃ*)—the people
- बोधयंती (*bodhayaṃtī*)—awakening
- अग्रे (*agre*)—in front of the sun
- सुगान् (*sugān*)—easy to travel
- पथः (*pathaḥ*)—paths
- कृण्वती (*kṛṇvatī*)—making
- याति (*yāti*)—she goes
- बृहद्रथा (*bṛhadrathā*)—she who has a chariot of huge form
- बृहती (*bṛhatī*)—she who is of great form
- विश्वमिन्वा (*viśvaminvā*)—she who pervades the world
- उषाः (*uṣāḥ*)—Uṣas
- अह्नां (*ahnāṃ*)—of the days
- अग्रे (*agre*)—in the first parts (in the morning)
- ज्योतिः (*jyotiḥ*)—brilliance
- यच्छति (*yacchati*)—she spreads

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Uṣas, who has an attractive form, goes awakening the people and making, in front of the sun, paths easy to travel. Uṣas, who has a chariot of huge form, is of great form and pervades the world, spreads brilliance in the first parts of the days (in the morning).

##### English translation
The lovely dawn, arousing man, goes before (the sun) preparing practicable paths, riding in a spacious chariot; vast, expanding everywhere, she diffuses light at the commencement of the days.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ए॒षा गोभि॑ररु॒णेभि॑र्युजा॒नास्रे॑धंती र॒यिमप्रा॑यु चक्रे।  
> प॒थो रदं॑ती सुवि॒ताय॑ दे॒वी पु॑रुष्टु॒ता वि॒श्ववा॑रा॒ वि भा॑ति॥

### Printed p. 171

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> एषा । गोभिः । अरुणेभिः । युजाना । आस्रेधंती । रयिं । अप्रआयु । चक्रे ।  
> पथः । रदंती । सुविताय । देवी । पुरुस्तुता । विश्ववारा । वि । भाति ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> एषोषा अरुणेभिररुणवर्णैर्गोभिर्बलीवर्दै रथं युजाना योजयंत्यस्रेधंत्यशुष्यंत्यक्षीणा वा । किं । रयिं धनमप्राय्यप्रगंतृ अविचलितं चक्रे ।
> करोति। किं कुर्वती । पथो मार्गान् रदंती प्रकाशयंती । किमर्थं । सुविताय
> सुष्टु गमनाय देवी द्योतमाना पुरुष्टुता बहुभिः स्तुता विश्ववारा सर्वैर्वरणीया वि भाति । प्रकाशं करोति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

This Uṣas, harnessing to the chariot the oxen of ruddy colour, अस्रेधंती (*asredhaṃtī*), unwithering, or unwasting. What does she do? रयिं (*rayiṃ*), wealth, अप्रायु (*aprāyu*), that which does not go away, unshaken, she makes. Doing what? रदंती (*radaṃtī*), making the paths shine; for what purpose? सुविताय (*suvitāya*), for good going. देवी (*devī*), shining; पुरुष्टुता (*puruṣṭutā*), praised by many; विश्ववारा (*viśvavārā*), to be chosen by all, she shines forth; she makes light.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- एषा (*eṣā*)—this goddess Uṣas
- अरुणेभिः (*aruṇebhiḥ*)—with golden-coloured
- गोभिः (*gobhiḥ*)—oxen
- युजाना (*yujānā*)—harnessing her chariot
- अस्रेधंती (*asredhaṃtī*)—without weariness
- अप्रायु (*aprāyu*)—firm
- रयिं (*rayiṃ*)—wealth
- चक्रे (*cakre*)—she makes
- देवी (*devī*)—she who is shining
- पुरुष्टुता (*puruṣṭutā*)—she who is praised by many
- विश्ववारा (*viśvavārā*)—she who is cherished by all, Uṣas
- सुविताय (*suvitāya*)—so as to be fit for excellent travel
- पथः (*pathaḥ*)—paths
- रदंती (*radaṃtī*)—making them shine
- वि भाति (*vi bhāti*)—she shines

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Uṣas, harnessing golden-coloured oxen to her chariot, without weariness, makes wealth firm. Uṣas, who is shining, praised by many and cherished by all, shines, making the paths shine so as to be fit for the excellent travel of the sun.

##### English translation
Harnessing the purple oxen to her car, unwearied she renders riches perpetual: a goddess, praised of many, and cherished by all, she shines, manifesting the paths that lead to good.

### Printed p. 172

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ए॒षा व्ये॑नी भवति द्वि॒बर्हा॑ आविष्कृण्वा॒ना त॒न्वं॑ पु॒रस्ता॑त् ।  
> ऋ॒तस्य॒ पंथा॒मन्वे॑ति सा॒धु प्र॑जान॒तीव॒ न दिशो॑ मिनाति ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> एषा । विएनी । भवति । द्विबर्हाः । आनिःकृण्वाना । तन्वं । पुरस्तात् ।  
> यतस्य । पंथां । अनु । एति । साधु । प्रजानतीइव । न । दिशः । मिनाति ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> एषोषा व्येनी भवति विशेषेण श्वेता भवति द्विबर्हा द्वयोः प्रथममध्यमयोः परिवृढोषाः पश्चात्तन्वं स्वीयां तनूमाविष्कृण्णाना प्रकटीकुर्वती ।
> कुत्र । पुरस्तात् पूर्वस्यां दिशि । किंच ऋतस्यादित्यस्य पंथां पंथानं साधु सम्यगन्वेति । किंच प्रजानतीव विश्वं प्रज्ञापयंती । इवेति संप्रत्यर्थे । न दिशो मिनाति ।
> न हिनस्ति । प्रत्युत दिशः प्रकाशयति । अथवा प्रजानतीवानुगंतव्यमिति प्रकर्षेणावगच्छंती चर्तस्य पंथामन्वेति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

This Uṣas becomes व्येनी (*vyenī*), especially white. द्विबर्हा (*dvibarhā*): of the two, the first and the middle, Uṣas being परिवृढोषाः (*parivṛḍhoṣāḥ*), **NOTE** afterwards, तनूमाविष्कृण्णाना (*tanūmāviṣkṛṇṇānā*), making her own body manifest. Where? पुरस्तात् (*purastāt*), in the eastern quarter. Moreover, she follows well the path of ऋतस्य (*ṛtasya*), of the sun. Moreover, प्रजानतीव (*prajānatīva*), as if knowing, making the world aware; the word इव (*iva*) is in the sense of "now". न दिशो मिनाति (*na diśo mināti*): she does not injure the quarters; on the contrary she illumines the quarters. Or else, as if knowing clearly what is to be followed, she follows the path of ऋतस्य (*ṛtasya*).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- एषा (*eṣā*)—this goddess Uṣas
- द्विबर्हाः (*dvibarhāḥ*)—although she has traversed the two places, earth and atmosphere
- पुरस्तात् (*purastāt*)—only in the eastern direction
- तन्नं (*tannaṃ*)—her form
- आविष्कृण्वाना (*āviṣkṛṇvānā*)—making manifest
- व्येनी भवति (*vyenī bhavati*)—she is of a very bright colour
- साधु (*sādhu*)—the excellent path on which she has to travel
- प्रजानतीव (*prajānatīva*)—as if she knew well
- ऋतस्य (*ṛtasya*)—of the sun
- पंथां (*paṃthāṃ*)—the path
- अन्वेति (*anveti*)—she goes, following
- दिशः (*diśaḥ*)—the quarters
- न मिनाति (*na mināti*)—she does not injure

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Although Uṣas has traversed the two places, earth and atmosphere, she is of a very bright colour, making her form manifest only in the eastern direction. As if she knew well the excellent path on which she has to travel, she goes following the path of the sun. She does not injure any quarter.

### Printed p. 173

##### English translation
Lucidly white is she, occupying the two (regions, the upper and middle firmament), and manifesting her person from the east: she traverses the path of the sun as if knowing (his course), and harms not the quarters of the horizon.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ए॒षा शु॒भ्रान त॒न्वो॑ विदा॒नोर्ध्वेव॑ स्ना॒ती दृ॒शये॑ नो  
> अस्थात् ।  
> अप॒ द्वेषो॒ बाध॑माना॒ तमां॑स्यु॒षा दि॒वो दु॑हि॒ता ज्योति॒षागा॑त् ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> एषा । शुभ्रा । न । तन्वः । विदाना । ऊर्ध्वा इव । स्नाती । दृशये । नः । अस्थात् ।  
> अप । द्वेषः । बाधमाना । तमांसि । उषाः । दिवः । दुहिता । ज्योतिषा । आ । अगात् ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> एषोषाः शुभ्रा न शुभ्रवर्णा निर्मला स्वलंकृता योषिदिव तन्नोऽंगानि
> विदाना प्रज्ञापयंती स्नाती स्नानं कुर्वाणोर्ध्वेवोन्नतेव स्नानादुत्तिष्ठंतीव
> नोऽस्मदर्थमस्माकं पुरतो वा दृशये सर्वेषां दर्शनायोदस्थात् । पूर्वस्यां
> दिश्युत्तिष्ठति । किं कुर्वती । द्वेषो द्वेष्याणि तमांस्यप बाधमाना दिवो दुहितोषा
> ज्योतिषा तेजसा सहागात् । आगच्छति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

This Uṣas, like a woman pure white in colour, spotless and well adorned, making known her limbs, and like one who has bathed and rises up from the bath, has risen for our sight, that is, before us, so that all may see her; she rises in the eastern quarter. What does she do? Driving away the hateful darknesses, Uṣas, the daughter of heaven, comes with radiance, that is, with brilliance.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- एषा (*eṣā*)—this goddess Uṣas
- शुभ्रा न (*śubhrā na*)—like a woman adorned with bright garments and ornaments
- तन्नः (*tannaḥ*)—her limbs
- विदाना (*vidānā*)—showing
- स्नाती (*snātī*)—like a woman who has gone down to bathe
- ऊर्ध्वेव (*ūrdhveva*)—appearing as if rising up from the water, swaying modestly
- नः (*naḥ*)—our
- दृशये (*dṛśaye*)—for the sight
- अस्थात् (*asthāt*)—she has risen and stands
- दिवः (*divaḥ*)—of the atmosphere

### Printed p. 174

- दुहिता (*duhitā*)—the daughter
- उषाः (*uṣāḥ*)—the goddess Uṣas
- द्वेषः (*dveṣaḥ*)—hateful
- तमांसि (*tamāṃsi*)—the dense darknesses
- अप बाधमाना (*apa bādhamānā*)—afflicting and driving them far away
- ज्योतिषा (*jyotiṣā*)—with her own brilliance
- आगात् (*āgāt*)—she comes

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

This goddess Uṣas, like a woman adorned with bright garments and ornaments, showing her limbs, and like a woman who has gone down to bathe and rises up from the water swaying modestly, appearing, has risen and stands for our sight. Uṣas, the daughter of the atmosphere, afflicting the hateful dense darknesses and driving them far away, comes with her own brilliance.

##### English translation
Exhibiting her person like a well-attired female, she stands before our eyes, (gracefully) inclining like (a woman who has been). bathing : dispersing the hostile glooms, Ushas, the daughter of heaven, comes with radiance.

> ए॒षा प्र॑ती॒ची दु॑हि॒ता दि॒वो नृन्नोषे॑व भ॒द्रा नि रि॑णीते॒  ॑
> अप्सः ।
